### Implementation

The MCP integration for Accommodation Service is failing. The error message indicates that the tool "check_accommodation_availability" is not available to the Accommodation feature. This suggests that the tool is not registered or available in the system. To resolve this issue, the tool should be registered and available in the system.

### Review

**Strengths:**
- The Accommodation Service has a robust implementation with multiple checks passing, including file checks, registry contract checks, and schema checks.
- The service has a well-defined registry contract with a clear set of declared and expected methods.
- The schema checks for various methods are passing, indicating a well-structured data model.

**Risk:**
- The MCP integration is failing due to a tool not being available to the Accommodation feature.
- The error message indicates that the tool "check_accommodation_availability" is not registered or available in the system.
- The implementation assessment suggests that the tool should be registered and available in the system to resolve the issue.

**Correction:**
- The tool "check_accommodation_availability" should be registered and available in the system.
- The Accommodation Service implementation should be reviewed to ensure that all required tools are registered and available.

**Retest:**
- The MCP integration should be retested after registering and making the tool available in the system.
- The Accommodation Service implementation should be re-evaluated to ensure that all checks are passing and the service is functioning as expected.

### Limitations

- Read-only sample calls; write tools are registration-only
- Direct MCP is not an authenticated UI test
- Account backend boundary uses the existing trusted X-Customer-ID contract, not browser authentication
